`accounts`\n\n| Field | Type | Notes |\n|-------|------|-------|\n| account | TEXT PK | Account code, 1 to 12 characters of A-Z and 0-9. |\n| name | TEXT NOT NULL | 1 to 40 characters, no comma or newline. |\n\n## `categories`\n\n| Field | Type | Notes |\n|-------|------|-------|\n| category | TEXT PK | Category code, 1 to 12 characters of A-Z and 0-9. |\n| description | TEXT NOT NULL | 1 to 40 characters, no comma or newline. |\n\n## `transactions`\n\n| Field | Type | Notes |\n|-------|------|-------|\n| txn_id | INTEGER PK | Kept from a load file, or one more than the largest present. |\n| date | TEXT NOT NULL | Calendar date written YYYY-MM-DD. |\n| account | TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES accounts(account) | Owning account. |\n| category | TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES categories(category) | Category. |\n| amount | INTEGER NOT NULL | Integer cents, never zero. |\n| memo | TEXT NOT NULL | 0 to 60 characters, no comma or newline. |\n\n- INDEX (account, date)\n- INDEX (date, account)\n\n## Migrations\n\nEach relational change has one staged record in `migrations/MIGRATION-<AccessPatternId>.md`.\nThe record carries Alembic `upgrade`/`downgrade` snippets and progresses from `planned` to `built`\nonly when its DATABASE build story completes. Alembic records deployment application separately.\n\n| Access Pattern | Revision | State |\n|----------------|----------|-------|\n\n## Config (.env)\n\n| Key | Type | Required | Notes codes alone.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nbound = \"ZZCLR\"\nlow = \"ZZCLQ\"\nkept_name = \"Db Kept\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=low, db.accounts.clear(min_account=bound)\n    assert isinstance(removed, int)\n    assert removed >= 2\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=\"ZZCLR1\") is None\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=\"ZZCLR2\") is None\n    kept = db.accounts.get(account=low)\n    assert kept is not None\n    assert kept.name == kept_name\n=== END AC accounts-clear ===\n\n=== AC
